📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: International Journal of Infectious Diseases  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-04  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.ijid.2026.109103  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 109103
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

混合与匹配增强剂 疫苗:马来西亚 队列研究 中针对 Omicron 的纵向体液免疫动力学。

Mixing versus matching booster vaccines: A longitudinal humoral immune kinetics against Omicron in a Malaysian cohort.
作者： Ashwathy Varadarajan Thundakattil, Rekha Prabhu, Girish Prabhu, Muthuvel Mani, Somsubhra De, Htoo Htoo Kyaw Soe, Charlotte Patrick Arjunan, Sumandeep Kaur Chahl, Debban A.L Ramesh, Ng Jian Chen
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估马来西亚 Omicron 暴发 期间加强治疗后的纵向体液 免疫应答,解决了解不同 疫苗 策略如何影响针对该变异体的免疫力的需要。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性 队列研究 研究对马来西亚 268 名医疗保健专业人员和医学生进行了为期 24 周的跟踪研究,他们接受了各种加强剂 疫苗(包括同源和异源治疗方案)。
核心方法
该研究使用 ADVIA Centaur 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 化学发光免疫分析和所有加强 疫苗 类型的 Omicron 变体的替代病毒中和测试评估了抗 S IgG 和抗 RBD 反应。
主要结果
与同源加强方案相比,异源方案,尤其是 IV-mR,显示出显着更高且持续的抗 S IgG 和抗 RBD 抗体反应。24 周内,观察到 IV-mR 的血清阳性率为 100%,而 IV-IV 同源加强剂将抗 S IgG 和抗 RBD 抗体的血清阳性率分别降低至 15.63% 和 18.75%。第 24 周时,Omicron 中和活性分别为 95.92% (IV-mR) 和 83.87% (VV-mR),而 IV-IV 组则低至 9.38%。
解释与创新
作者解释说,异源加强剂 疫苗 诱导了针对 Omicron 的优越且延长的体液 疫苗,强调了这些发现在 疫苗 策略背景下的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,异源加强方案可能更有效地维持对 Omicron 的免疫力,这可以为未来的 疫苗接种 策略和公共卫生建议提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有将年龄、性别或种族视为潜在的混杂因素,这可能会限制研究结果的普遍性。由于关注特定的 队列研究 和有限的混杂变量控制,证据是中等的。

Mixing versus matching booster vaccines: A longitudinal humoral immune kinetics against Omicron in a Malaysian cohort.

Authors: Ashwathy Varadarajan Thundakattil, Rekha Prabhu, Girish Prabhu, Muthuvel Mani, Somsubhra De, Htoo Htoo Kyaw Soe, Charlotte Patrick Arjunan, Sumandeep Kaur Chahl, Debban A.L Ramesh, Ng Jian Chen
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: The objective of our study was to evaluate the longitudinal humoral immune response following booster regimens in Malaysia during the Omicron outbreak. METHODS: In this prospective cohort study, 268 healthcare professionals and medical students in Malaysia were followed up for 24 weeks following homologous (mRNA-mRNA (mR-mR), viral vector-viral vector (VV-VV), inactivated virus-inactivated virus (IV-IV) and heterologous (inactivated virus-mRNA (IV-mR), viral vector-mRNA (VV-mR) booster vaccines. Of them, 190 recipients underwent longitudinal immunogenicity study. Anti-S IgG and anti-RBD response were assessed by ADVIA Centaur SARS-CoV-2 chemiluminescent immunoassay followed by surrogate virus neutralization test using Omicron-variant across all boosters. RESULTS: Compared to homologous boosters, heterologous regimens, particularly the IV-mR booster, elicited significantly higher and sustained anti-S IgG and anti-RBD antibody (P<0.0001) responses. Over 24 weeks, the heterologous IV-mR booster had 100% seropositive anti-S IgG and anti-RBD antibodies, conversely, in the homologous (IV-IV) booster, the seropositivity of anti-S IgG and anti-RBD antibody reduced to 15.63% and 18.75% respectively. Omicron-virus neutralizing activity was observed as high as 95.92% (IV-mR) and 83.87% (VV-mR) following W24 of the heterologous booster, while virus neutralization was found as low as 9.38% in the IV-IV homologous booster. Age, gender, and ethnicity were not found as confounding factors for antibody kinetics following booster vaccine. CONCLUSION: In conclusion, heterologous booster vaccines induced superior prolonged humoral-immune response against COVID-19 infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the longitudinal humoral immune response following booster regimens in Malaysia during the Omicron outbreak, addressing the need to understand how different vaccine strategies affect immunity against the variant.
Design and population
This prospective cohort study followed 268 healthcare professionals and medical students in Malaysia for 24 weeks after receiving various booster vaccines, including homologous and heterologous regimens.
Core methods
The study assessed anti-S IgG and anti-RBD responses using ADVIA Centaur SARS-CoV-2 chemiluminescent immunoassay and surrogate virus neutralization test with the Omicron variant across all booster vaccine types.
Main results
Heterologous regimens, especially IV-mR, showed significantly higher and sustained anti-S IgG and anti-RBD antibody responses compared to homologous boosters. Over 24 weeks, 100% seropositivity was observed for IV-mR, while IV-IV homologous booster had reduced seropositivity to 15.63% and 18.75% for anti-S IgG and anti-RBD antibodies, respectively. Omicron-neutralizing activity was 95.92% (IV-mR) and 83.87% (VV-mR) at week 24, whereas it was as low as 9.38% in the IV-IV group.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that heterologous booster vaccines induced superior and prolonged humoral immune responses against Omicron, highlighting the novelty of these findings in the context of vaccine strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that heterologous booster regimens may be more effective for maintaining immunity against Omicron, which could inform future vaccination strategies and public health recommendations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study did not account for age, gender, or ethnicity as potential confounding factors, which may limit the generalizability of the findings. The evidence is moderate due to the focus on a specific cohort and limited confounding variable control.

高通量芯片筛选使DNA适配体快速适应严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 进化。

High-Throughput On-Chip Screening Enables Rapid Adaptation of DNA Aptamers to SARS-CoV-2 Evolution.
作者： Yujie He, Zhenglin Yang, Yu-An Kuo, Yuting Wu, Diego Fonseca-Albert, Kyle K Le, Jeffrey Guo, Yanxing Wang, Anh-Thu Nguyen, Yuan-I Chen
摘要中文翻译快速病原体 进化 通过侵蚀 疫苗、治疗和诊断工具的有效性来威胁公众健康。尽管刺突蛋白靶向单克隆抗体 (mAb) 在最初的 暴发 的 10-12 个月内开发出来,作为关键的治疗诊断剂,但它们的重新设计一直难以跟上病毒 进化 的步伐,导致许多中和抗体无效。在这里,我们展示了一个高通量适体工程平台,该平台将随机理性混合文库多样化与重新调整用途的 MiSeq 筛选相结合,以针对新兴的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 尖峰变体快速重新编程适体。几天内就分析了 3 种不同的 刺突蛋白 和 11,792 个独特的适体变体设计之间的相互作用(从池扩增到筛选分析的单次运行)。从最初针对野生型 (WT) 刺突蛋白 选择的 40 nt 适体开始,我们的筛选鉴定了亲和力提高 4 倍的 Delta 结合突变体和将不可检测的结合转化为纳摩尔亲和力的 Omicron 结合突变体。我们还鉴定了一种 WT 选择性突变体,其对 Delta 的亲和力大大降低,并且有助于尖峰识别的碱基。将高通量结合数据与分子动力学模拟相结合,进一步有助于合理化变体特异性适体-刺突相互作用背后的序列依赖性结构特征。最后,我们开发了基于 WT 和 Omicron 选择性突变体的荧光链位移传感器,能够高度特异性地检测 刺突蛋白 变体,并具有强大的性能。总之,这些发现证明了一个快速、序列解析的适体工程平台,可以使适体适应不断进化的病原体。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管刺突蛋白靶向单克隆抗体 (mAb) 在最初的 暴发 的 10-12 个月内开发出来,作为关键的治疗诊断剂,但它们的重新设计一直难以跟上病毒 进化 的步伐,导致许多中和抗体无效。
设计与对象

核心方法
所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
从最初针对野生型 (WT) 刺突蛋白 选择的 40 nt 适体开始,我们的筛选鉴定了亲和力提高 4 倍的 Delta 结合突变体和将不可检测的结合转化为纳摩尔亲和力的 Omicron 结合突变体。
解释与创新
最后,我们开发了基于 WT 和 Omicron 选择性突变体的荧光链位移传感器,能够高度特异性地检测 刺突蛋白 变体,并具有强大的性能。
科研与公卫意义
快速病原体 进化 通过侵蚀 疫苗、治疗和诊断工具的有效性来威胁公众健康。
局限与证据强度
总之,这些发现证明了一个快速、序列解析的适体工程平台,可以使适体适应不断进化的病原体。

High-Throughput On-Chip Screening Enables Rapid Adaptation of DNA Aptamers to SARS-CoV-2 Evolution.

Authors: Yujie He, Zhenglin Yang, Yu-An Kuo, Yuting Wu, Diego Fonseca-Albert, Kyle K Le, Jeffrey Guo, Yanxing Wang, Anh-Thu Nguyen, Yuan-I Chen
Original abstract (English)
Rapid pathogen evolution threatens public health by eroding the effectiveness of vaccines, therapeutics, and diagnostic tools. Although spike-protein-targeting monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) were developed within 10-12 months of the initial outbreak to serve as key theranostic agents, their redesign has struggled to keep pace with viral evolution, rendering many neutralizing antibodies ineffective. Here, we demonstrate a high-throughput aptamer engineering platform that combines a random-rational hybrid library diversification with repurposed MiSeq screening to rapidly reprogram aptamers against emerging SARS-CoV-2 spike variants. Interactions between 3 different spike proteins and 11,792 unique aptamer variant designs were profiled within days (a single run from pool amplification to screen analysis). Starting from a 40-nt aptamer originally selected against wild-type (WT) spike protein, our screen identified a Delta-binding mutant with a 4-fold affinity improvement and an Omicron-binding mutant that converted undetectable binding into nanomolar affinity. We also identified a WT-selective mutant with substantially reduced affinity for Delta as well as bases that contribute to spike recognition. Integrating high-throughput binding data with molecular dynamics simulations further helped to rationalize the sequence-dependent structural features underlying variant-specific aptamer-spike interactions. Finally, we developed fluorescent strand-displacement sensors based on both WT- and Omicron-selective mutants, enabling highly specific detection of spike protein variants with robust performance. Together, these findings demonstrate a rapid and sequence-resolved aptamer engineering platform for adapting aptamers to evolving pathogens.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Although spike-protein-targeting monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) were developed within 10-12 months of the initial outbreak to serve as key theranostic agents, their redesign has struggled to keep pace with viral evolution, rendering many neutralizing antibodies ineffective.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RBD 变体的高通量无细胞分析可实现快速、定量的体外亲和力图谱绘制。

High-throughput cell-free profiling of SARS-CoV-2 RBD variants enables rapid and quantitative in vitro affinity landscape mapping.
作者： Jiami Han, Laura Grasemann, Julia Tischler, Fatemeh Arefi, Maria Andrea Gonzales Castillo, Edward B. Irvine, NingNing Chen, Sai T. Reddy, Sebastian J. Maerkl
摘要中文翻译严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体继续威胁公共健康,因此需要研究受体结合域 (RBD) 突变 对抗体逃避的累积和上位效应。我们提出了一个结合无细胞蛋白质合成和微流体的高通量平台,以量化大量 RBD 三联体突变体(涵盖野生型和 Omicron 之间的进化空间)对两种治疗抗体和一种工程结合剂的亲和力。利用快速体外基因组装和无细胞合成,我们表达了 518 个 RBD 变体并获得了 31,740 个定量亲和力测量结果,以生成三种综合结合能图谱。这种方法能够实现快速、大规模的体外亲和力分析和基于机器学习的预测,为研究新兴变异提供了宝贵的工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体继续威胁公共健康,因此需要研究受体结合域 (RBD) 突变 对抗体逃避的累积和上位效应。
设计与对象
我们提出了一个结合无细胞蛋白质合成和微流体的高通量平台,以量化大量 RBD 三联体突变体(涵盖野生型和 Omicron 之间的进化空间)对两种治疗抗体和一种工程结合剂的亲和力。
核心方法
利用快速体外基因组装和无细胞合成,我们表达了 518 个 RBD 变体并获得了 31,740 个定量亲和力测量结果,以生成三种综合结合能图谱。
主要结果
这种方法能够实现快速、大规模的体外亲和力分析和基于机器学习的预测,为研究新兴变异提供了宝贵的工具。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为证明了他们的平台在理解 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体的结合特性方面的实用性,该平台在高通量无细胞分析用于亲和景观图谱的应用方面是新颖的。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过快速评估新兴 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体中的抗体逃避潜力,为 监测 策略和治疗干预措施的制定提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其体外性质而受到限制,可能无法完全反映体内情况,并且由于缺乏临床或流行病学数据,证据不充分。

High-throughput cell-free profiling of SARS-CoV-2 RBD variants enables rapid and quantitative in vitro affinity landscape mapping.

Authors: Jiami Han, Laura Grasemann, Julia Tischler, Fatemeh Arefi, Maria Andrea Gonzales Castillo, Edward B. Irvine, NingNing Chen, Sai T. Reddy, Sebastian J. Maerkl
Original abstract (English)
SARS-CoV-2 variants continue to threaten public health, necessitating the study of cumulative and epistatic effects of receptor-binding domain (RBD) mutations on antibody evasion. We present a high-throughput platform combining cell-free protein synthesis and microfluidics to quantify the affinity of a large number of RBD triplet mutants covering the evolutionary space between the wild type and Omicron against two therapeutic antibodies and one engineered binder. Using rapid in vitro gene assembly and cell-free synthesis, we expressed 518 RBD variants and obtained 31,740 quantitative affinity measurements to generate three comprehensive binding energy landscapes. This approach enables rapid and large-scale in vitro affinity profiling and machine-learning-based predictions, providing a valuable tool for studying emerging variants.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
SARS-CoV-2 variants continue to threaten public health, necessitating the study of cumulative and epistatic effects of receptor-binding domain (RBD) mutations on antibody evasion.
Design and population
We present a high-throughput platform combining cell-free protein synthesis and microfluidics to quantify the affinity of a large number of RBD triplet mutants covering the evolutionary space between the wild type and Omicron against two therapeutic antibodies and one engineered binder.
Core methods
Using rapid in vitro gene assembly and cell-free synthesis, we expressed 518 RBD variants and obtained 31,740 quantitative affinity measurements to generate three comprehensive binding energy landscapes.
Main results
This approach enables rapid and large-scale in vitro affinity profiling and machine-learning-based predictions, providing a valuable tool for studying emerging variants.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret their findings as demonstrating the utility of their platform for understanding the binding properties of SARS-CoV-2 variants, which is novel in its application of high-throughput cell-free profiling for affinity landscape mapping.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of surveillance strategies and therapeutic interventions by enabling rapid assessment of antibody evasion potential in emerging SARS-CoV-2 variants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its in vitro nature, which may not fully reflect in vivo conditions, and the evidence is moderate due to the absence of clinical or epidemiological data.

Journal: Molecular Biomedicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-03  |  DOI: 10.1186/s43556-026-00560-y  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 7, 1, 158
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

基于关键突变体和KP.3 RBDs的二聚体mRNA 疫苗诱导广谱免疫并保护小鼠免受JN.1和XDV挑战。

A dimeric mRNA vaccine based on key-mutant and KP.3 RBDs induces broad-spectrum immunity and protects mice against JN.1 and XDV challenges.
作者： Jiali Xu, Rui Peng, Longhai Yuan, Huan Zhang, Cong Tang, Yanan Zhou, Hao Yang, Yun Yang, Qing Huang, Junbin Wang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 Omicron 亚变体的持续抗原性 进化 逐渐侵蚀了 疫苗 引发的保护性免疫,从而推动了对下一代候选药物的需求,这些候选药物可针对系统发育上不同的菌株提供广谱保护。
设计与对象
在这里,我们报告了 SV 的设计和临床前评估,SV 是一种编码异二聚体受体结合域 (RBD) 抗原的 mRNA 疫苗。在此构建体中,先前优化的单体 RBD (BSCOV06) 与 KP.3 RBD 串联连接,在单个免疫原内呈现两个抗原性不同的 RBD。
核心方法
BALB/c 小鼠中的两剂 SV 方案引发了高滴度中和抗体,对 BA.1、XBB.1.5、JN.1、KP.3 和系统发育较远的 XDV 变体具有强交叉反应性。
主要结果
整合的 B 细胞受体 (BCR) 和 T 细胞受体 (TCR) 谱图分析表明,相对于 BSCOV06,SV 驱动着性质不同的适应性免疫重塑。
解释与创新
主要特征包括类别转换体细胞超突变的增加、持续的幼稚 B 细胞参与、广泛的多克隆 T 细胞扩增以及跨两个淋巴细胞区室的广泛 VJ 基因使用重编程。
科研与公卫意义
在 BALB/c 和 K18-hACE2 转基因小鼠中,SV 赋予针对 JN.1 和 XDV 攻击的强大保护作用,显着降低肺部病毒载量并减轻组织病理学损伤。
局限与证据强度
SV 实现了与三剂量 BSCOV06 方案相当或超过的免疫原性和交叉保护功效,支持异二聚体抗原设计的潜力。

A dimeric mRNA vaccine based on key-mutant and KP.3 RBDs induces broad-spectrum immunity and protects mice against JN.1 and XDV challenges.

Authors: Jiali Xu, Rui Peng, Longhai Yuan, Huan Zhang, Cong Tang, Yanan Zhou, Hao Yang, Yun Yang, Qing Huang, Junbin Wang
Original abstract (English)
The continued antigenic evolution of SARS-CoV-2 Omicron subvariants has progressively eroded vaccine-elicited protective immunity, driving demand for next-generation candidates that confer broad-spectrum protection against phylogenetically divergent strains. Here we report the design and preclinical evaluation of SV, an mRNA vaccine encoding a heterodimeric receptor-binding domain (RBD) antigen. In this construct, a previously optimized monomeric RBD (BSCOV06) is tandemly linked to the KP.3 RBD, presenting two antigenically distinct RBDs within a single immunogen. A two-dose SV regimen in BALB/c mice elicited high-titer neutralizing antibodies with potent cross-reactivity against BA.1, XBB.1.5, JN.1, KP.3, and the phylogenetically distant XDV variant. Integrated B cell receptor (BCR) and T cell receptor (TCR) repertoire profiling revealed that SV drives qualitatively distinct adaptive immune remodeling relative to BSCOV06. Key features included elevated class-switched somatic hypermutation, sustained naive B cell engagement, broad polyclonal T cell expansion, and extensive VJ gene-usage reprogramming across both lymphocyte compartments. In BALB/c and K18-hACE2 transgenic mice, SV conferred robust protection against JN.1 and XDV challenge, substantially reducing pulmonary viral loads and attenuating histopathological injury. Notably, SV achieved immunogenicity and cross-protective efficacy comparable to or exceeding those of the three-dose BSCOV06 schedule, supporting the potential of heterodimeric antigen design. These findings support SV as a promising broad-spectrum COVID-19 vaccine candidate. More broadly, they suggest that heterodimeric RBD architectures incorporating antigenically divergent variants may represent a generalizable platform for countering viral immune evasion, with implications for future SARS-CoV-2 variants and other rapidly evolving viral pathogens.

不同 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 免疫史中的长期抗体减弱和抗体相关保护

Long-term antibody waning and antibody-associated protection across diverse SARS-CoV-2 immune histories
作者： Koki Numakura, Sho Miyamoto, Akiko Sataka, Haruko Takeyama, Tadaki Suzuki
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在表征长期抗体减弱及其与不同免疫史(包括 疫苗接种 和感染组合)中 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染保护的关系。
设计与对象
2021 年 12 月至 2023 年 3 月期间,对日本 25,800 名成年人进行了四次全国性血清流行病学调查,进行了一项重复横断面研究。该研究重点关注具有不同免疫史的个体,包括未感染者和 Omicron 突破性感染者。
核心方法
测量了针对祖先刺突受体结合结构域的总抗体和针对 Omicron BA.5 的中和抗体滴度。使用贝叶斯分层模型根据 疫苗 剂量数、感染史、Omicron 适应的二价 疫苗接种、年龄、性别和 疫苗 产品来估计抗体减弱。
主要结果
Among uninfected individuals, three to five vaccine doses produced higher anti-spike antibody titers than two doses, while antibody kinetics after the third or later doses were broadly similar. Relative to titers 30 days after the third dose, titers in the three-, four-, or five-dose groups declined to 0.18-fold (95% credible interval [CredI], 0.16–0.19), 0.19-fold (95% CredI, 0.13–0.26), or 0.21-fold (95% CredI, 0.13–0.33) at 1 year and 0.10-fold (95% CredI, 0.07–0.14), 0.14-fold (95% CredI, 0.06–0.24), or 0.14-fold (95% CredI, 0.05–0.29) at 2 years.
解释与创新
与单独使用 疫苗接种 相比,Omicron 突破性感染与更高且更持久的抗尖峰和 BA.5 中和抗体滴度相关。这些发现为评估具有不同免疫史的人群的长期抗体减弱和加强时机提供了一个定量框架。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为下一代 疫苗接种 策略的开发提供信息,该策略诱导针对循环 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体的更广泛、更持久的抗体反应。它们还提供了有关保护持续时间以及 Omicron 突破性感染在塑造 免疫应答 方面的潜在益处的见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于血清流行病学调查,不包括直接的临床结果。由于该研究的观察性质以及存在未测量的混杂因素的可能性,证据强度适中。

Long-term antibody waning and antibody-associated protection across diverse SARS-CoV-2 immune histories

Authors: Koki Numakura, Sho Miyamoto, Akiko Sataka, Haruko Takeyama, Tadaki Suzuki
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background SARS-CoV-2 vaccination and infection generate diverse immune histories, but antibody waning beyond 1 year remains insufficiently characterized. We estimated 2-year antibody trajectories and antibody-associated protection across different combinations of vaccination and infection history. Methods We conducted a repeated cross-sectional study using four nationwide seroepidemiological surveys of 25,800 adults in Japan between December 2021 and March 2023. We measured total antibodies against the ancestral spike receptor-binding domain and neutralizing antibody titers against Omicron BA.5. Bayesian hierarchical models estimated antibody waning according to vaccine dose number, infection history, Omicron-adapted bivalent vaccination, age, sex, and vaccine product. Antibody trajectories were linked to an antibody-risk model to estimate protection against symptomatic infection. Results Here we show that, among uninfected individuals, three to five vaccine doses produce higher anti-spike antibody titers than two doses, while antibody kinetics after the third or later doses are broadly similar. Relative to titers 30 days after the third dose, titers in the three-, four-, or five-dose groups decline to 0.18-fold (95% credible interval [CredI], 0.16–0.19), 0.19-fold (95% CredI, 0.13–0.26), or 0.21-fold (95% CredI, 0.13–0.33) at 1 year and 0.10-fold (95% CredI, 0.07–0.14), 0.14-fold (95% CredI, 0.06–0.24), or 0.14-fold (95% CredI, 0.05–0.29) at 2 years. Omicron breakthrough infection is associated with higher and more durable anti-spike and BA.5 neutralizing antibody titers than vaccination alone. Based on anti-spike antibody trajectories, estimated 90% protection against symptomatic BA.5 infection lasts 99 days (95% CredI, 89–108) after three doses and 116 days (95% CredI, 111–121) after four or five doses in uninfected individuals, but 276 days (95% CredI, 245–319) to 450 days (95% CredI, 343 to >730) in Omicron-infected individuals. Hypothetical neutralization-escape scenarios substantially shorten estimated protection. Conclusions These findings provide a quantitative framework for evaluating long-term antibody waning and booster timing across populations with diverse immune histories, and may inform the development of next-generation vaccination strategies that induce broader, more durable antibody responses against circulating SARS-CoV-2 variants.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to characterize long-term antibody waning and its association with protection against SARS-CoV-2 infections across diverse immune histories, including vaccination and infection combinations.
Design and population
A repeated cross-sectional study was conducted using four nationwide seroepidemiological surveys of 25,800 adults in Japan between December 2021 and March 2023. The study focused on individuals with varying immune histories, including those uninfected and those with Omicron breakthrough infections.
Core methods
Total antibodies against the ancestral spike receptor-binding domain and neutralizing antibody titers against Omicron BA.5 were measured. Bayesian hierarchical models were used to estimate antibody waning based on vaccine dose number, infection history, Omicron-adapted bivalent vaccination, age, sex, and vaccine product.
Main results
Among uninfected individuals, three to five vaccine doses produced higher anti-spike antibody titers than two doses, while antibody kinetics after the third or later doses were broadly similar. Relative to titers 30 days after the third dose, titers in the three-, four-, or five-dose groups declined to 0.18-fold (95% credible interval [CredI], 0.16–0.19), 0.19-fold (95% CredI, 0.13–0.26), or 0.21-fold (95% CredI, 0.13–0.33) at 1 year and 0.10-fold (95% CredI, 0.07–0.14), 0.14-fold (95% CredI, 0.06–0.24), or 0.14-fold (95% CredI, 0.05–0.29) at 2 years.
Interpretation and novelty
Omicron breakthrough infection is associated with higher and more durable anti-spike and BA.5 neutralizing antibody titers than vaccination alone. The findings provide a quantitative framework for evaluating long-term antibody waning and booster timing across populations with diverse immune histories.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results may inform the development of next-generation vaccination strategies that induce broader, more durable antibody responses against circulating SARS-CoV-2 variants. They also offer insights into the duration of protection and the potential benefits of Omicron breakthrough infections in shaping immune responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on seroepidemiological surveys and does not include direct clinical outcomes. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study and the potential for unmeasured confounding factors.

从序列到策略:通过遗传距离早期检测新的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变异,以减少住院治疗

From sequences to strategies: Early detection of new SARS-CoV-2 variants via genetic distance to reduce hospitalizations
作者： Marika D’Avanzo, Aung Pone Myint, Giacomo Cacciapaglia, Stefan Hohenegger, Francesco Conventi, Marta C. Nunes, Marika D'Avanzo
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
我们从这个综合框架中得出的研究结果强调了在评估与新出现变异相关的住院风险时遗传距离指标和公共卫生干预措施的潜在相关性。
设计与对象
这项研究扩展了基于 Levenshtein 距离的无监督聚类算法,以跟踪和预测 2020 年至 2024 年 1 月六个欧洲国家的变异优势。
核心方法
深度神经网络 (DNN) 经过训练,将新兴链分类为可能占主导地位的链,同时 CatBoost 模型评估重要变量,并通过模拟探索修改 疫苗 遗传距离、遏制措施和 疫苗接种 覆盖范围。
主要结果
基于模型的模拟表明,涉及改进的 疫苗 匹配或更强的遏制措施的场景与较低的预测住院负担相关。
解释与创新
仅将 疫苗接种 覆盖率加倍的效果很小,但与严格遏制相结合时,效果会进一步降低。
科研与公卫意义
The COVID-19 pandemic highlighted the critical need for robust methods to monitor viral evolution and detect emerging variants of concern (VOCs).
局限与证据强度
连续链之间以及循环菌株和 疫苗 菌株之间的遗传距离指标是与住院模式相关的最具信息性的变量之一。

From sequences to strategies: Early detection of new SARS-CoV-2 variants via genetic distance to reduce hospitalizations

Authors: Marika D’Avanzo, Aung Pone Myint, Giacomo Cacciapaglia, Stefan Hohenegger, Francesco Conventi, Marta C. Nunes, Marika D'Avanzo
Original abstract (English)
The COVID-19 pandemic highlighted the critical need for robust methods to monitor viral evolution and detect emerging variants of concern (VOCs). This study expanded an unsupervised clustering algorithm, based on Levenshtein distance, to track and predict variant predominance across six European countries from 2020 to January 2024. We also investigated the influence of genetic distances and containment strategies on hospitalization rates. Spike protein sequences were transformed into temporal chains. A deep neural network (DNN) was trained to classify emerging chains as likely dominant, while a CatBoost model assessed important variables, and simulations explored modifying vaccine genetic distance, containment measures, and vaccination coverage. Approximately 5,000 sequences per week enabled early chain detection within four weeks. The DNN achieved high classification performance for identifying future predominant chains within 3–4 weeks of detection. Genetic distance metrics between consecutive chains and between circulating and vaccine strains were among the most informative variables associated with hospitalization patterns. Model-based simulations suggested that scenarios involving improved vaccine matching or stronger containment measures were associated with lower predicted hospitalization burdens. Doubling vaccination coverage alone had minimal effect but showed additional reductions when combined with strict containment. Our findings from this integrated framework highlight the potential relevance of genetic distance metrics and public health interventions when assessing hospitalization risk associated with emerging variants.
View research seven-element analysis

Our findings from this integrated framework highlight the potential relevance of genetic distance metrics and public health interventions when assessing hospitalization risk associated with emerging variants.

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
Genetic distance metrics between consecutive chains and between circulating and vaccine strains were among the most informative variables associated with hospitalization patterns.

Impaired memory B-cell formation after mRNA-based COVID-19 booster vaccination in patients with inflammatory bowel disease receiving anti-TNF treatment

Impaired memory B-cell formation after mRNA-based COVID-19 booster vaccination in patients with inflammatory bowel disease receiving anti-TNF treatment
作者： Paul A. Gill, Lachlan R. Bradbury, Alina Wang, Julie Hogg, Kathryn Demase, McKenzie, J., Holly A. Fryer, Daryl Geers, Luca M. Zaeck, Irene Boo
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景接受抗TNF 治疗治疗慢性炎症性疾病的患者表现出抗体反应受损,但尚不清楚免疫记忆的形成是否受到影响。
设计与对象
We evaluated antibody responses and memory B cells (Bmem) after COVID-19 booster vaccination in inflammatory bowel disease (IBD) patients receiving anti-TNF treatment. Blood was sampled at baseline, 1, and 6 months after WH1/BA.5 bivalent or XBB.1.5 monovalent vaccination from 27 IBD patients receiving intravenous anti-TNF and 44 controls.
核心方法
使用感染性病毒测定来测量中和抗体。通过 ELISA 对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 刺突受体结合域 (RBD) 特异性血清 IgG 进行定量,并使用来自祖先、Omicron BA.1、BA.5、XBB.1.5 和 JN.1 变体的重组蛋白通过流式细胞术对 RBD 特异性 Bmem 进行免疫表型分析。
主要结果
疫苗 RBD 患者的血清 IgG 和中和抗体在 疫苗 治疗后 1 个月前有所增加,但低于对照组。疫苗接种 后,祖先、BA.5 和 XBB.1.5 特异性 Bmem 增加,但患者中的 Bmem 显着低于对照组。在 RBD 特异性 Bmem 中,疫苗接种 后最近激活的 CD21lo 细胞的频率增加,并且患者中的频率高于对照组。
解释与创新
疫苗接种 后,患者中抗原特异性 Bmem 表达 IgG4 的情况较少,而表达 IgG3 或 IgD 的情况较多。在 疫苗接种 之后,更多 RBD 特异性 Bmem 识别了多种病毒变体。
科研与公卫意义
这支持了每年加强 疫苗接种 以预防严重疾病和病毒传播的建议。疫苗 治疗后 1 个月和 6 个月时,患者的 疫苗 特异性抗体反应和记忆 B 细胞形成显着低于对照组,表明峰值反应和持久性较差。
局限与证据强度
然而,与对照组相比,患者的可与亚变体结合的 Bmem 较少。尽管存在多个 疫苗接种,但来自患者的 疫苗 特异性记忆 B 细胞表现出表型改变,并且对多个 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 Omicron 亚变体的识别能力降低,这可能是患者生发中心反应受损的结果。

Impaired memory B-cell formation after mRNA-based COVID-19 booster vaccination in patients with inflammatory bowel disease receiving anti-TNF treatment

Authors: Paul A. Gill, Lachlan R. Bradbury, Alina Wang, Julie Hogg, Kathryn Demase, McKenzie, J., Holly A. Fryer, Daryl Geers, Luca M. Zaeck, Irene Boo
Original abstract (English)
BackgroundPatients receiving anti-TNF treatment for chronic inflammatory disease display impaired antibody responses, but it remains unclear how immune memory formation is affected. We evaluated antibody responses and memory B cells (Bmem) after COVID-19 booster vaccination in inflammatory bowel disease (IBD) patients receiving anti-TNF treatment. MethodologyBlood was sampled at baseline, 1, and 6 months after WH1/BA.5 bivalent or XBB.1.5 monovalent vaccination from 27 IBD patients receiving intravenous anti-TNF and 44 controls. Neutralizing antibodies were measured using an infectious virus assay. SARS-CoV-2 spike receptor binding domain (RBD)-specific serum IgG was quantified by ELISA, and RBD-specific Bmem were immunophenotyped by flow cytometry using recombinant proteins from ancestral, Omicron BA.1, BA.5, XBB.1.5, and JN.1 variants. ResultsSerum IgG to vaccine RBD and neutralizing antibodies in patients increased pre to 1 month post-vaccination, but were lower than controls. Ancestral-, BA.5- and XBB.1.5-specific Bmem increased after vaccination but were significantly lower in patients than controls. Within RBD-specific Bmem, frequencies of recently activated CD21lo cells were increased after vaccination, and were higher in patients than controls. Fewer antigen-specific Bmem in patients expressed IgG4, and more expressed IgG3 or IgD following vaccination. Following vaccination, more RBD-specific Bmem recognized multiple viral variants. However, patients had fewer Bmem that could bind to subvariants than controls. ConclusionAntibody and Bmem responses to COVID-19 booster vaccination in anti-TNF-treated IBD patients displayed reduced capacity, durability and cross-reactivity, suggesting impaired immune memory for protection against breakthrough infection. This supports the recommendation for annual booster vaccination to prevent severe disease and viral spread. HighlightsO_LIIBD patients on anti-TNF biologics mount antibody responses and form vaccine-specific memory B cells following WH1/BA.5 bivalent or XBB.1.5 monovalent mRNA COVID-19 booster vaccination. C_LIO_LIVaccine-specific antibody responses and memory B cell formation in patients are significantly lower than in controls at 1- and 6 months after vaccination, indicative of poorer peak response and durability. C_LIO_LIDespite multiple vaccinations, vaccine-specific memory B cells from patients display phenotypic alterations and reduced recognition of multiple SARS-CoV-2 Omicron subvariants, potentially a consequence of impaired germinal center responses in patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
BackgroundPatients receiving anti-TNF treatment for chronic inflammatory disease display impaired antibody responses, but it remains unclear how immune memory formation is affected.
Design and population
We evaluated antibody responses and memory B cells (Bmem) after COVID-19 booster vaccination in inflammatory bowel disease (IBD) patients receiving anti-TNF treatment. Real-world healthcare resource utilization and cost burden of COVID-19 in commercially insured adults at high-risk of severe disease in the US: a retrospective database analysis.

Real-world healthcare resource utilization and cost burden of COVID-19 in commercially insured adults at high-risk of severe disease in the US: a retrospective database analysis.
作者： Tendai Mugwagwa, Benjamin A Abramoff, Siddharth Karanth, Tram Nham, Ravi K Goyal, Elizabeth Esterberg, Gurinder S Sidhu, Alon Yehoshua, Ruth Mokgokong
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
High-risk patients with COVID-19 remained at elevated risk of severe outcomes in the Omicron era, even after receiving multiple vaccine doses.
设计与对象
We report the Omicron-era healthcare resource utilization (HCRU) and cost burden of COVID-19 among commercially insured, oral antiviral therapy-eligible patients at high risk of severe disease. Eligible commercially insured patients were aged ≥18 years, had a COVID-19 diagnosis between 1 July 2023 and 30 June 2024, and had ≥1 risk factor for severe COVID-19.
核心方法
This retrospective analysis used the TriNetX Linked US database. All-cause and COVID-19-related HCRU and costs were assessed during acute (0-30 days from diagnosis) and post-acute (31 days until December 31, 2024) follow-up.
主要结果
在43,487名符合条件的患者中,56.2%的患者年龄≥50岁,85.6%的患者患有1-5种高危病症。9.8% 的患者因 ≥1 次全因住院普通病房,平均每位患者 (PP) 总费用为 14,715 美元(急性期,10,833 美元 PP;急性期后,14,403 美元 PP)。1.3% 的患者因各种原因入住重症监护病房 (ICU),平均 PP 总费用为 75,371 美元(急性期,62,882 美元 PP;急性期后,71,666 美元 PP)。在 ICU 需要机械通气的少数患者中,平均全因 PP 成本总体为 135,677 美元 PP(急性期,106,577 美元 PP;急性期后,130,309 美元 PP)。
解释与创新
During the Omicron era, HCRU and costs remained substantial and increased with disease severity among commercially insured patients at high risk of severe COVID-19.
科研与公卫意义
These findings are crucial to inform economic evaluations and payer decision‐making regarding COVID‐19 antiviral therapies.
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告该研究的局限性。

Real-world healthcare resource utilization and cost burden of COVID-19 in commercially insured adults at high-risk of severe disease in the US: a retrospective database analysis.

Authors: Tendai Mugwagwa, Benjamin A Abramoff, Siddharth Karanth, Tram Nham, Ravi K Goyal, Elizabeth Esterberg, Gurinder S Sidhu, Alon Yehoshua, Ruth Mokgokong
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND AIM: High-risk patients with COVID-19 remained at elevated risk of severe outcomes in the Omicron era, even after receiving multiple vaccine doses. We report the Omicron-era healthcare resource utilization (HCRU) and cost burden of COVID-19 among commercially insured, oral antiviral therapy-eligible patients at high risk of severe disease. METHODS: This retrospective analysis used the TriNetX Linked US database. Eligible commercially insured patients were aged ≥18 years, had a COVID-19 diagnosis between 1 July 2023 and 30 June 2024, and had ≥1 risk factor for severe COVID-19. All-cause and COVID-19-related HCRU and costs were assessed during acute (0-30 days from diagnosis) and post-acute (31 days until December 31, 2024) follow-up. RESULTS: Of the 43,487 eligible patients, 56.2% were aged ≥50 years and 85.6% had 1-5 high-risk conditions. Patients were followed for a mean of 11.4 months following COVID-19 diagnosis (acute phase, 1.0 month; post-acute phase, 10.6 months). 13,499 patients (31.0%) received treatment for COVID-19. 9.8% of patients had ≥1 all-cause inpatient general ward admission, with a mean per-patient (PP) total cost of $14,715 (acute phase, $10,833 PP; post-acute phase, $14,403 PP). 1.3% of patients had an all-cause intensive care unit (ICU) admission, with a mean PP total cost of $75,371 (acute phase, $62,882 PP; post-acute phase, $71,666 PP). Among the small number of patients requiring mechanical ventilation in the ICU, mean all-cause PP costs were $135,677 PP overall (acute phase, $106,577 PP; post-acute phase, $130,309 PP). 28.4% of patients had ≥1 all-cause emergency department visit (mean total cost, $1,434 PP; acute phase, $842 PP; post-acute phase, $1,408 PP). CONCLUSIONS: During the Omicron era, HCRU and costs remained substantial and increased with disease severity among commercially insured patients at high risk of severe COVID-19. These findings are crucial to inform economic evaluations and payer decision‐making regarding COVID‐19 antiviral therapies.

喜马偕尔邦(印度)严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2奥密克戎谱系新兴亚分支的分子流行病学:一项横断面研究

Molecular Epidemiology of Emerging Subvariants of the SARS-CoV-2 Omicron Lineage: A Cross-sectional Study from Himachal Pradesh, India
作者： Priyanka Rao, Sunite A Ganju, Lata R. Chandel
摘要中文翻译引言:严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2(严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2)表现出持续的遗传变异,导致全球范围内出现多种病毒谱系。这些变异株可能影响病毒的传播能力、致病潜力和免疫逃逸,从而改变疾病模式。基因组监测(基因组监测)允许系统追踪病毒变异株和流行变种,因此监测对于制定知情的公共卫生计划和临床准备至关重要。目的:检测印度喜马偕尔邦山区奥密克戎谱系变种的出现情况。材料与方法:本横断面研究于2024年1月至6月在印度喜马偕尔邦曼迪的Shri Lal Bahadur Shastri医学院进行。所有来自该州四个病毒研究与诊断实验室(VRDLs)的Ct值≤30的严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2阳性样本均被纳入研究。使用Q-Line分子病毒RNA提取试剂盒进行RNA提取,并在牛津纳米孔MinION平台上进行全基因组测序(Whole 基因组 Sequencing,WGS)。使用Medaka和Nanopolish生成共识序列,用于变异株识别和系统发育分析。所有人口统计学和临床数据,包括年龄、性别、临床表现和血清学状态,均使用Microsoft Excel 2021记录并整理。研究聚焦于生成的基因组序列,数据以数字和百分比形式呈现。结果:共收到30例严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2阳性样本。在严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2阳性病例中,成年女性占比最高,为17例(56.7%),其中大多数患者年龄在19-60岁之间,共17例(56.7%),且大多数样本来自Shimla,共11例(36.7%)。研究发现,19例(63.3%)为JN.1亚谱系,是感染的主要原因。大多数病例(20例,66.7%)报告至门诊部门,其中35%(7例)表现出轻度症状,如发热和咳嗽。尽管有23例(76.7%)接种了疫苗,但仍有10例(33.3%)需要住院治疗,且未报告任何关注变异株(关注变异株)。结论:追踪严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2变异株显示,奥密克戎的JN.1亚谱系在本地流行,且临床表现通常较轻。持续的严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2遗传变异强调了将基因组监测(基因组监测)与流行病学数据整合的必要性,以实现新兴变异株的早期检测并加强控制策略。研究问题与背景:严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2的持续遗传变异推动了新谱系的出现,这些新谱系可能对传播能力、致病性和免疫逃逸产生潜在影响,因此需要监测流行变异株以支持公共卫生规划。本研究旨在检测印度喜马偕尔邦奥密克戎谱系亚变种的出现,填补了该地区基因组监测(基因组监测)方面的空白。研究设计与人群:本横断面研究于2024年1月至6月在印度喜马偕尔邦曼迪的Shri Lal Bahadur Shastri医学院进行。所有来自该州四个病毒研究与诊断实验室(VRDLs)的Ct值≤30的严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2阳性样本均被纳入研究。方法:使用Q-Line分子病毒RNA提取试剂盒进行RNA提取,并在牛津纳米孔MinION平台上进行全基因组测序(Whole 基因组 Sequencing,WGS)。使用Medaka和Nanopolish生成共识序列,用于变异株识别和系统发育分析。主要结果:共收到30例严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2阳性样本,其中成年女性占56.7%(17/30),大多数患者年龄在19-60岁之间(56.7%,17/30)。样本主要来自Shimla(36.7%,11/30),其中63.3%(19/30)的病例被鉴定为JN.1亚谱系。大多数病例(66.7%,20/30)报告至门诊部门,其中35%(7/20)表现出轻度症状,如发热和咳嗽。尽管有76.7%(23/30)的病例接种了疫苗,但仍有33.3%(10/30)需要住院治疗,且未报告任何关注变异株(关注变异株)。解释与创新性:在测序病例中,JN.1亚谱系的主导地位表明奥密克戎亚变种在本地持续传播,且临床表现通常较轻。本研究强调了基因组监测(基因组监测)在区域环境下检测新兴变异株的实用性。科学与公共卫生意义:这些发现支持将基因组监测(基因组监测)与流行病学数据整合,以实现新兴严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2变异株的早期检测,并加强山区地区的本地控制策略。JN.1亚谱系的主导地位突显了需要针对性的公共卫生应对措施。局限性与证据强度:由于样本量较小(n=30)和单中心设计,研究结果的推广性受到限制,且依赖Ct值≤30可能引入选择偏倚。由于横断面研究设计和缺乏对混杂因素的控制,证据强度中等。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在检测印度喜马偕尔邦 Omicron 谱系变异的出现,因为 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 显示出持续的遗传变异,导致全球范围内出现多种病毒谱系。这些 突变 可以影响病毒的传播性、致病潜力和免疫逃逸,从而导致疾病模式的改变。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究于 2024 年 1 月至 2024 年 6 月在印度喜马偕尔邦曼迪 Shri Lal Bahadur Shastri 医学院进行。包括从该邦四个病毒研究和诊断实验室 (VRDL) 收到的全邦所有 Ct 值≤30 的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 阳性样本。
核心方法
使用Q-Line分子病毒RNA提取试剂盒进行核糖核酸(RNA)提取,并在Oxford Nanopore MinION平台上进行全基因组测序(WGS)。使用 Medaka 和 Nanopolish 生成共有序列,用于变异鉴定和系统发育分析。
主要结果
共收到30份严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2阳性样本。在严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2阳性病例中,成年女性所占比例最高,为17例(56.7%),大多数患者年龄为19-60岁,为17例(56.7%),大多数样本来自西姆拉,为11例(36.7%)。该研究确定 19 例 (63.3%) 中的 JN.1 亚系是感染的主要原因。
解释与创新
追踪 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体揭示了 Omicron 的 JN.1 亚系占主导地位,症状通常较轻。严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的连续遗传 进化 强调需要将稳健、持续的 基因组监测 与流行病学数据相结合,以便及早发现新出现的变异并加强控制策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,基因组监测 对于追踪 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的新兴变体(特别是 JN.1 亚系)的重要性,并支持将此类数据与流行病学信息相整合,以加强公共卫生准备和控制策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其横截面设计和对特定地理区域的关注而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。证据是中等的,因为它基于样本量有限的单一研究,并且没有详细的统计分析。

Molecular Epidemiology of Emerging Subvariants of the SARS-CoV-2 Omicron Lineage: A Cross-sectional Study from Himachal Pradesh, India

Authors: Priyanka Rao, Sunite A Ganju, Lata R. Chandel
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Severe Acute Respiratory Syndrome Coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) shows continuous genetic variation, resulting in the emergence of multiple viral lineages worldwide. These mutations can influence viral transmissibility, pathogenic potential, and immune escape, resulting in changed disease pattern. Genomic surveillance allows systematic tracking of viral evolution and circulating variants; thus, monitoring is essential for informed public health planning and clinical preparedness. Aim: To detect the emergence of variants of Omicron lineage in the hilly state of Himachal Pradesh, India. Materials and Methods: This cross-sectional study was conducted at the Shri Lal Bahadur Shastri Medical College, Mandi, Himachal Pradesh, India from January 2024 to June 2024. All the SARS-CoV-2 positive samples with Ct value ≤30 received across the state from the four Virus Research and Diagnostic Laboratories (VRDLs) of the state were included. Ribonucleic acid (RNA) extraction was performed using the Q-Line Molecular Viral RNA Extraction Kit, and Whole Genome Sequencing (WGS) was carried out on the Oxford Nanopore MinION platform. Consensus sequences were generated using Medaka and Nanopolish for variant identification and phylogenetic analysis. All demographic and clinical data, like age, sex, clinical presentation, and vaccination status, were recorded and tabulated in Microsoft Excel 2021. The study focused on genomic sequences generated, and the data were presented as numbers and percentages. Results: A total of 30 SARS-CoV-2 positive samples were received. Among SARS-CoV-2 positive cases, adult females constituted the highest proportion, 17 (56.7%), with most patients aged 19-60 years, 17 (56.7%), and the majority of samples originating from Shimla, 11 (36.7%). The study identified JN.1 sublineages in 19 (63.3%) as the predominant cause of infection. Most cases, 20 (66.7%) reported to the outpatient department. Mild symptoms such as fever and cough were seen in 7 (35%) cases. Even though 23 (76.7%) of cases were vaccinated with the COVID-19 vaccine, 10 (33.3%) required admissions. However, no mortality was reported. Conclusion: Tracking SARS-CoV-2 variants revealed the predominance of the JN.1 sublineage of Omicron with generally mild symptoms. Continuous genetic evolution of SARS CoV-2 highlights the need for robust, ongoing genomic surveillance integrated with epidemiological data to enable early detection of emerging variants and strengthen control strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to detect the emergence of variants of the Omicron lineage in Himachal Pradesh, India, as SARS-CoV-2 shows continuous genetic variation, resulting in the emergence of multiple viral lineages worldwide. These mutations can influence viral transmissibility, pathogenic potential, and immune escape, resulting in changed disease patterns.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study was conducted at the Shri Lal Bahadur Shastri Medical College, Mandi, Himachal Pradesh, India from January 2024 to June 2024. All the SARS-CoV-2 positive samples with Ct value ≤30 received across the state from the four Virus Research and Diagnostic Laboratories (VRDLs) of the state were included.
Core methods
Ribonucleic acid (RNA) extraction was performed using the Q-Line Molecular Viral RNA Extraction Kit, and Whole Genome Sequencing (WGS) was carried out on the Oxford Nanopore MinION platform. Consensus sequences were generated using Medaka and Nanopolish for variant identification and phylogenetic analysis.
Main results
A total of 30 SARS-CoV-2 positive samples were received. Among SARS-CoV-2 positive cases, adult females constituted the highest proportion, 17 (56.7%), with most patients aged 19-60 years, 17 (56.7%), and the majority of samples originating from Shimla, 11 (36.7%). The study identified JN.1 sublineages in 19 (63.3%) as the predominant cause of infection.
Interpretation and novelty
Tracking SARS-CoV-2 variants revealed the predominance of the JN.1 sublineage of Omicron with generally mild symptoms. Continuous genetic evolution of SARS-CoV-2 highlights the need for robust, ongoing genomic surveillance integrated with epidemiological data to enable early detection of emerging variants and strengthen control strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of genomic surveillance for tracking emerging variants of SARS-CoV-2, particularly the JN.1 sublineage, and support the integration of such data with epidemiological information to enhance public health preparedness and control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and the focus on a specific geographic region, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a single study with a limited sample size and no detailed statistical analysis.

Costs and healthcare resource utilization after COVID-19 hospitalization among US Medicare patients: a retrospective claims analysis.

Costs and healthcare resource utilization after COVID-19 hospitalization among US Medicare patients: a retrospective claims analysis.
作者： Alon Yehoshua, Rachel M. Black, An-An Zhou, Michelle A. Silver, Yun Han, Santiago M C Lopez, Benjamin Yarnoff, Rajeev M Nepal, Maria M. Fernandez, Mohammad Ashraf Chaudhary
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to address the economic burden of post-COVID-19 hospitalization on healthcare systems by quantifying direct healthcare costs and resource utilization in Medicare beneficiaries during the Omicron period.
设计与对象
This retrospective observational study analyzed 100% Medicare FFS claims data from beneficiaries aged ≥65 years who had a COVID-19 hospitalization between September 2023 and September 2024, with continuous enrollment for six months pre-admission and post-discharge.
核心方法
采用组内前/后设计来比较入院前和出院后六个月期间每名患者的平均累积医疗费用和 HCRU,不包括指数住院费用。结果根据住院严重程度和出院后护理环境进行分层。
主要结果
在72,053名受益人(平均年龄80.8岁;55.1%女性)中,96.5%有≥1项高风险条件。出院后每位患者的平均总医疗费用比入院前增加了13,808美元(p < 0.001),主要由护理机构/家庭护理(8,461美元)和住院费用(4,278美元)驱动。需要侵入性机械通气的患者(21,122美元)、住院天数≥7天的患者(23,223美元)以及被转至医疗设施的患者(28,857美元)的费用增加幅度更大(所有p < 0.001)。每位患者的平均总医疗索赔数量增加了6.1次,住院天数增加了3.6天(两者p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting the substantial economic burden of post-COVID-19 hospitalization, particularly among patients with greater severity and post-acute care needs, which is a novel insight into the long-term financial impact of the disease.
科研与公卫意义
The findings inform the need for targeted post-discharge care strategies to manage the sustained economic burden of COVID-19 in older adults, particularly those with severe illness or requiring extended care.
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对索赔数据的依赖而受到限制,该数据可能无法捕获所有医疗资源的利用或结果。证据有力,结果具有统计显着性(p < 0.001)且样本量大。

Costs and healthcare resource utilization after COVID-19 hospitalization among US Medicare patients: a retrospective claims analysis.

Authors: Alon Yehoshua, Rachel M. Black, An-An Zhou, Michelle A. Silver, Yun Han, Santiago M C Lopez, Benjamin Yarnoff, Rajeev M Nepal, Maria M. Fernandez, Mohammad Ashraf Chaudhary
Original abstract (English)
AIMS: The objective of this study was to quantify all-cause direct healthcare costs and healthcare resource utilization (HCRU) in the six months before and after COVID-19-associated hospitalization among Medicare fee-for-service (FFS) beneficiaries during the recent Omicron period (March 2023-March 2025). METHODS: This retrospective observational study used 100% Medicare FFS claims data. Beneficiaries aged ≥65 years with a COVID-19 hospitalization between September 2023 and September 2024 and continuous enrollment for six months pre-admission and post-discharge were included. A within-group pre/post design compared mean cumulative per-patient healthcare costs and HCRU during the six-month pre-admission and post-discharge periods, excluding index hospitalization costs. Outcomes were stratified by hospitalization severity and post-discharge care setting. RESULTS: Among 72,053 beneficiaries (mean age 80.8 years; 55.1% female), 96.5% had ≥1 high-risk condition. Mean total healthcare costs increased by $13,808 per patient post-discharge versus pre-admission (p < 0.001), driven primarily by care facility/home health ($8,461) and inpatient ($4,278) costs. Larger increases were observed among patients requiring invasive mechanical ventilation ($21,122), with index stays ≥7 days ($23,223), and those discharged to healthcare facilities ($28,857) (all p < 0.001). Mean total healthcare claims increased by 6.1 per patient and inpatient length of stay by 3.6 days (both p < 0.001). CONCLUSIONS: COVID-19 hospitalization among Medicare FFS beneficiaries is associated with substantial increases in post-discharge healthcare costs and utilization, particularly among patients with greater severity and post-acute care needs. These findings highlight the sustained economic burden of COVID-19 in older adults and underscore the need for targeted post-discharge care strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the economic burden of post-COVID-19 hospitalization on healthcare systems by quantifying direct healthcare costs and resource utilization in Medicare beneficiaries during the Omicron period.
Design and population
This retrospective observational study analyzed 100% Medicare FFS claims data from beneficiaries aged ≥65 years who had a COVID-19 hospitalization between September 2023 and September 2024, with continuous enrollment for six months pre-admission and post-discharge.
Core methods
A within-group pre/post design was used to compare mean cumulative per-patient healthcare costs and HCRU during the six-month pre-admission and post-discharge periods, excluding index hospitalization costs. Outcomes were stratified by hospitalization severity and post-discharge care setting.
Main results
Among 72,053 beneficiaries (mean age 80.8 years; 55.1% female), 96.5% had ≥1 high-risk condition. Mean total healthcare costs increased by $13,808 per patient post-discharge versus pre-admission (p < 0.001), driven primarily by care facility/home health ($8,461) and inpatient ($4,278) costs. Larger increases were observed among patients requiring invasive mechanical ventilation ($21,122), with index stays ≥7 days ($23,223), and those discharged to healthcare facilities ($28,857) (all p < 0.001). Mean total healthcare claims increased by 6.1 per patient and inpatient length of stay by 3.6 days (both p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting the substantial economic burden of post-COVID-19 hospitalization, particularly among patients with greater severity and post-acute care needs, which is a novel insight into the long-term financial impact of the disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for targeted post-discharge care strategies to manage the sustained economic burden of COVID-19 in older adults, particularly those with severe illness or requiring extended care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on claims data, which may not capture all healthcare resource utilization or outcomes. The evidence is strong, with statistically significant results (p < 0.001) and a large sample size.

Prognostic impact of right ventricular dilatation and echocardiography use criteria in hospitalized COVID-19 patients.

Prognostic impact of right ventricular dilatation and echocardiography use criteria in hospitalized COVID-19 patients.
作者： Arnold Méndez-Toro, Ingrid Tatiana Rojas-Ruiz, Luis Eduardo Silva-DiazGranados, Andrés Ruano-Cadena, Manuel Agustín Paz-Meneses, Ricardo Andrés Novoa-Alvarez
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The surge of severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 [coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19)] infection created unprecedented diagnostic and logistical pressures. Given the established potential for cardiac injury and the critical need to optimize scarce resources, establishing the clinical utility and prognostic value of transthoracic echocardiography (TTE) became imperative.
设计与对象
We performed a retrospective cohort study analyzing records from patients with confirmed COVID-19 who underwent TTE. Total 149 patients were analyzed. Median age was 66 years [interquartile range (IQR): 56-73], median hospital stay was 13 days (IQR: 6-23).
核心方法
收集了社会人口学、生化和超声心动图参数。死亡率是主要结局。我们评估了TTE指征的观察者间一致性(Kappa统计量),依据美国心脏病学会基金会2011年和美国超声心动学会2020年的指南,并评估了临床影响的确定(后续患者管理的改变)。
主要结果
总体和重症监护单元死亡率发生率分别为39.6%和60%。升高的生化标志物(白细胞、中性粒细胞、乳酸脱氢酶和C反应蛋白)与死亡率相关。关键的是,右心室(RV)扩张和/或应变(P = 0.008)被确定为唯一显著预测死亡率的超声心动图发现。对AUC分类的观察者间一致性较高(κ ≥ 0.798)。此外,TTE在79.7%的病例中促使了临床管理的改变。
解释与创新
RV pathology is a potent, quantifiable prognostic indicator. While AUC demonstrated high reliability, the significant and frequent changes in management suggest that the current guidelines may possess inherent limitations when addressing the unique, acute prognostic complexities of severe COVID-19 disease.
科研与公卫意义
The findings cautiously inform the importance of incorporating right ventricular assessment in the echocardiographic evaluation of hospitalized COVID-19 patients for improved risk stratification and clinical decision-making.
局限与证据强度
该研究基于回顾性数据,可能引入偏倚并限制因果关系的确立。由于研究性质为观察性且存在潜在混杂因素,证据强度为中等。

Prognostic impact of right ventricular dilatation and echocardiography use criteria in hospitalized COVID-19 patients.

Authors: Arnold Méndez-Toro, Ingrid Tatiana Rojas-Ruiz, Luis Eduardo Silva-DiazGranados, Andrés Ruano-Cadena, Manuel Agustín Paz-Meneses, Ricardo Andrés Novoa-Alvarez
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The surge of severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 [coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19)] infection created unprecedented diagnostic and logistical pressures. Given the established potential for cardiac injury and the critical need to optimize scarce resources, establishing the clinical utility and prognostic value of transthoracic echocardiography (TTE) became imperative. AIM: To evaluate the prognostic predictors and the adherence to appropriate use criteria (AUC) for TTE in a highly affected setting. METHODS: We performed a retrospective cohort study analyzing records from patients with confirmed COVID-19 who underwent TTE. Socio-demographic, biochemical, and echocardiographic parameters were collected. Mortality was the primary outcome. We assessed inter-observer agreement (Kappa statistic) for TTE indications (based on American College of Cardiology Foundation 2011 and American Society of Echocardiography 2020 guidelines) and the determination of clinical impact (a subsequent change in patient management). RESULTS: Total 149 patients were analyzed. Median age was 66 years [interquartile range (IQR): 56-73], median hospital stay was 13 days (IQR: 6-23). Overall and intensive care unit mortality rates were 39.6% and 60%, respectively. Elevated biochemical markers (leukocytes, neutrophils, lactate dehydrogenase, and C-reactive protein) were associated with mortality. Crucially, right ventricular (RV) dilatation and/or strain (P = 0.008) was identified as the sole echocardiographic finding significantly predictive of mortality. Inter-observer agreement for classifying AUC was high (κ ≥ 0.798). Furthermore, TTE prompted a change in clinical management in 79.7% of the cases. CONCLUSION: RV pathology is a potent, quantifiable prognostic indicator. While AUC demonstrated high reliability, the significant and frequent changes in management suggest that the current guidelines may possess inherent limitations when addressing the unique, acute prognostic complexities of severe COVID-19 disease.

Question and background
The surge of severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 [coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19)] infection created unprecedented diagnostic and logistical pressures. 用于开发广谱冠状病毒 疫苗 的工程 HR1-Stem Helix-HR2 三聚体平台。

An Engineered HR1-Stem Helix-HR2 Trimeric Platform for Developing Broad-Spectrum Coronavirus Vaccines.
作者： Xi-Kui Sun, Junhao Fan, Xiaolu Xie, Yu-Ting Shi, Xiaoqing Liu, Tiefeng Xu, Si-Yu Yang, Keda Shi, Cheng-Shu Xie, Shixiong Li
摘要中文翻译摘要 以 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 为代表的免疫逃避病毒变种的快速传播凸显了对广谱冠状病毒 疫苗 的迫切需求。尽管使用外源支架进行受体结合域(RBD)的多聚体展示可以改善免疫应答,但这种方法可能会引发针对支架本身的脱靶免疫应答,并且仍然受到快速RBD抗原漂移的限制。在这里,我们报告了自组装三聚体亚基 疫苗 的合理设计,称为 RBD-七肽重复序列 1 (HR1)-茎螺旋 (SH)-七肽重复序列 2 (HR2) (RHS),它通过优化的接头将 JN.1 RBD 与来自 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的高度保守的 SH 表位整合到天然 HR1-HR2 三聚体支架中。RHS 表现出高结构稳定性、高效三聚化和增强的抗原呈递。在小鼠中,RHS 引发强大的体液和细胞 免疫应答,包括针对多种 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体的有效交叉中和抗体和泛 β 冠状病毒 SH 特异性抗体。在 K18-hACE2 小鼠中,RHS 能够针对抗原匹配和抗原不匹配的 Omicron 变体提供强大的保护,而鼻内免疫可诱导有效的粘膜 IgA 和 IgG 反应。此外,模块化 RHS 平台很容易适应 SARS-CoV 和 MERS-CoV 的抗原,强调了其在泛冠状病毒 疫苗 开发中的多功能性。总之,这些发现将 RHS 确立为克服病毒免疫逃避和推进下一代 疫苗 设计的通用策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
以 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 为代表的免疫逃避病毒变种的快速传播凸显了对广谱冠状病毒 疫苗 的迫切需求。
设计与对象
该研究报告了自组装三聚体亚基 疫苗(称为 RHS)的合理设计,该亚基通过优化的连接子将 JN.1 RBD 与来自 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的高度保守的 SH 表位整合到天然 HR1-HR2 三聚体支架内。
核心方法
RHS 疫苗 的设计采用合理的方法,通过优化的接头将 JN.1 RBD 与来自 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的高度保守的 SH 表位整合到天然 HR1-HR2 三聚支架内。
主要结果
RHS 表现出高结构稳定性、高效三聚化和增强的抗原呈递。在小鼠中,RHS 会引发强大的体液和细胞 免疫应答,包括针对多种 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体的有效交叉中和抗体和泛 β 冠状病毒 SH 特异性抗体。
解释与创新
作者解释说,RHS 平台代表了一种通过整合保守表位与变体特异性抗原来开发广谱冠状病毒 疫苗 的新策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,RHS 可以为下一代 疫苗 的开发提供信息,该下一代 疫苗 能够克服免疫逃避并提供针对多种 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体的保护。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠体内实验,证据仅基于摘要报告,可能无法完全体现 疫苗 的临床相关性或长期疗效。

An Engineered HR1-Stem Helix-HR2 Trimeric Platform for Developing Broad-Spectrum Coronavirus Vaccines.

Authors: Xi-Kui Sun, Junhao Fan, Xiaolu Xie, Yu-Ting Shi, Xiaoqing Liu, Tiefeng Xu, Si-Yu Yang, Keda Shi, Cheng-Shu Xie, Shixiong Li
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The rapid spread of immune‐evasive viral variants, exemplified by SARS‐CoV‐2, highlights the urgent need for broad‐spectrum coronavirus vaccines. Although multimeric display of the receptor‐binding domain (RBD) using exogenous scaffolds improve immune responses, such approaches may elicit off‐target immune responses against the scaffolds themselves and remain limited by rapid RBD antigenic drift. Here, we report the rational design of a self‐assembling trimeric subunit vaccine, termed RBD‐heptad repeat 1 (HR1)‐stem helix (SH)‐heptad repeat 2 (HR2) (RHS), which integrates the JN.1 RBD with a highly conserved SH epitope from SARS‐CoV‐2 within a native HR1‐HR2 trimeric scaffold via optimized linkers. RHS exhibits high structural stability, efficient trimerization, and enhanced antigen presentation. In mice, RHS elicits robust humoral and cellular immune responses, including potent cross‐ neutralizing antibodies against diverse SARS‐CoV‐2 variants and pan‐betacoronavirus SH‐specific antibodies. In K18‐hACE2 mice, RHS confers strong protection against both antigen‐matched and antigen‐mismatched Omicron variants, while intranasal immunization induces potent mucosal IgA and IgG responses. Furthermore, the modular RHS platform is readily adaptable to antigens from SARS‐CoV and MERS‐CoV, underscoring its versatility for pan‐coronavirus vaccine development. Together, these findings establish RHS as a generalizable strategy for overcoming viral immune evasion and advancing next‐generation vaccine design.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The rapid spread of immune‐evasive viral variants, exemplified by SARS‐CoV‐2, highlights the urgent need for broad‐spectrum coronavirus vaccines.
Design and population
The study reports the rational design of a self‐assembling trimeric subunit vaccine, termed RHS, which integrates the JN.1 RBD with a highly conserved SH epitope from SARS‐CoV‐2 within a native HR1‐HR2 trimeric scaffold via optimized linkers.
Core methods
The RHS vaccine was designed using a rational approach to integrate the JN.1 RBD with a highly conserved SH epitope from SARS‐CoV‐2 within a native HR1‐HR2 trimeric scaffold via optimized linkers.
Main results
RHS exhibits high structural stability, efficient trimerization, and enhanced antigen presentation. In mice, RHS elicits robust humoral and cellular immune responses, including potent cross‐neutralizing antibodies against diverse SARS‐CoV‐2 variants and pan‐betacoronavirus SH‐specific antibodies.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the RHS platform represents a novel strategy for developing broad-spectrum coronavirus vaccines by integrating conserved epitopes with variant-specific antigens.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that RHS could inform the development of next-generation vaccines capable of overcoming immune evasion and providing protection against diverse SARS-CoV-2 variants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vivo experiments in mice, and the evidence is based on abstract-only reporting, which may not fully capture the clinical relevance or long-term efficacy of the vaccine.

Question and background
The COVID-19 pandemic triggered global non-pharmaceutical interventions (NPIs), and China combined strict containment with widespread SARS-CoV-2 vaccination, yet the resulting epidemiology of 13 common respiratory pathogens among acute respiratory infection (ARI) patients during and after containment adjustment remained incompletely characterized. This study aimed to analyze the epidemiological characteristics of these 13 respiratory pathogens in hospitalized ARI patients across the pandemic and post-adjustment periods from 2021 to 2025.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis was conducted on 9,674 hospitalized patients with acute respiratory infection at the study centre, all of whom were tested for 13 respiratory pathogens over the period 2021 to 2025, spanning the COVID-19 pandemic and the post-adjustment phase.
Core methods
All enrolled ARI patients underwent testing for 13 respiratory pathogens using fluorescence PCR-capillary electrophoresis, and the resulting detection data were retrospectively analyzed to characterize pathogen spectrum and coinfection patterns.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not report complete numerical results; it only indicates that lower respiratory tract infections (LRTIs) demonstrated a significantly higher prevalence and diagnostic yield than upper respiratory tract infections (URTIs) (LRTI vs.

自由放养的新热带蝙蝠中广泛传播的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染表明人与蝙蝠之间存在反复的溢出效应

Widespread SARS-CoV-2 infection in free-ranging Neotropical bats suggests repeated human-to-bat spillback
作者： Jorge Armijos-Rivera, Gabriela Parra, Marlon Bravo-Bonilla, María Gonzalez-Maldonado, Daniela Román-Cáceres, Adamary Vásquez-Tituana, Jessica Valdivieso, Freddy Tinoco, Alicia Villavicencio, Nikolay Aguirre
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 反复从人传给蝙蝠的可能性,因为已知蝙蝠携带多种冠状病毒,并被认为是人类病原体的祖先来源。
设计与对象
该研究对厄瓜多尔南部安第斯山脉和亚马逊生态系统中自由放养的新热带蝙蝠进行了采样,其中 126 只蝙蝠代表 9 个不同的类群。
核心方法
RT-qPCR 筛查用于检测蝙蝠样本中的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RNA,并从 5 个个体中回收了部分至接近完整的病毒 基因组 进行进一步分析。
主要结果
在 34.12% 的蝙蝠样本中检测到 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RNA,回收的病毒 基因组 显示出与当代人类 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 谱系 > 99% 的核苷酸同一性,聚类在多个全球系统发育分支内。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在蝙蝠中观察到的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染可能是由于反复的人为溢出,而不是蝙蝠种群内的持续循环。
科研与公卫意义
The results highlight the need to integrate wildlife surveillance into long-term strategies for monitoring and managing emerging infectious diseases, particularly in the context of the ongoing COVID-19 pandemic.
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,并且结果基于相对较小的样本量,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。然而,考虑到蝙蝠来源的 基因组 与人类 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 谱系的核苷酸高度同一性,证据是强有力的。

Widespread SARS-CoV-2 infection in free-ranging Neotropical bats suggests repeated human-to-bat spillback

Authors: Jorge Armijos-Rivera, Gabriela Parra, Marlon Bravo-Bonilla, María Gonzalez-Maldonado, Daniela Román-Cáceres, Adamary Vásquez-Tituana, Jessica Valdivieso, Freddy Tinoco, Alicia Villavicencio, Nikolay Aguirre
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Bats harbor exceptional coronavirus diversity and are considered ancestral sources of several human pathogens. As SARS-CoV-2 transitioned from pandemic emergence to global endemicity in humans, concern has shifted from wildlife-to-human spillover toward reverse zoonosis. However, infection of free-ranging bat populations under natural conditions has not previously been demonstrated. Here, we report widespread detection of SARS-CoV-2 RNA in wild Neotropical bats sampled across Andean and Amazonian ecosystems of Southern Ecuador. RT-qPCR screening of 126 individuals, representing nine taxa, detected SARS-CoV-2 RNA in 34.12% of bats across multiple sites. Partial to near-complete viral genomes recovered from five individuals showed >99% nucleotide identity to contemporary human SARS-CoV-2 lineages and clustered within multiple global phylogenetic clades. Mixed-effects modeling revealed pronounced species-level heterogeneity, a positive association between elevation and infection probability, and higher infection probability in females compared with males. The close phylogenetic affinity of bat-derived genomes to circulating human variants and their distribution across multiple lineages suggest repeated anthropogenic spillback rather than sustained bat-specific circulation. These results expand current understanding of the ecological footprint of the COVID-19 pandemic and highlight the importance of integrating wildlife surveillance into long-term One Health strategies for emerging infectious diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the potential for repeated human-to-bat spillback of SARS-CoV-2, as bats are known to harbor diverse coronaviruses and are considered ancestral sources of human pathogens.
Design and population
The study involved sampling free-ranging Neotropical bats across Andean and Amazonian ecosystems in Southern Ecuador, with 126 individuals representing nine different taxa.
Core methods
RT-qPCR screening was used to detect SARS-CoV-2 RNA in bat samples, and partial to near-complete viral genomes were recovered from five individuals for further analysis.
Main results
SARS-CoV-2 RNA was detected in 34.12% of the sampled bats, and the recovered viral genomes showed >99% nucleotide identity to contemporary human SARS-CoV-2 lineages, clustering within multiple global phylogenetic clades.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the observed SARS-CoV-2 infections in bats are likely due to repeated anthropogenic spillback rather than sustained circulation within bat populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the need to integrate wildlife surveillance into long-term strategies for monitoring and managing emerging infectious diseases, particularly in the context of the ongoing COVID-19 pandemic.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on a relatively small sample size, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. However, the evidence is strong given the high nucleotide identity of the bat-derived genomes to human SARS-CoV-2 lineages.

在基利菲整合监测揭示了疫情后持续的严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2传播以及新兴JN.1亚系的免疫逃逸

Integrated surveillance in Kilifi reveals continued SARS-CoV-2 circulation and immune escape of emerging JN.1 sublineages post-pandemic
作者： Gloria Konyino, Bernadette Kutima, Sharon Owuor, Donwilliams Omuoyo, Gerald Waweru, Igor Andrade Santos, Arnold W. Lambisia, Esther Nyadzua Katama, Angela Maina, Joyce Uche Nyiro
摘要中文翻译引言 疫情后严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2在非洲被优先级降低。肯尼亚的疫苗接种研究仅使用基于武汉的疫苗,结果显示其对奥密克戎及其亚系的中和效果降低。方法 我们在2024年11月至2026年3月期间,在基利菲健康人口统计和监测系统内的五个医疗机构中进行了严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2免疫监测。招募了出现急性呼吸道症状的患者,并采集了鼻咽/咽拭子进行严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RT-PCR检测。从30名经确认感染严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2且RT-PCR检测呈阳性并具有基因组序列的患者中采集了康复期血浆,用于针对感染变异株LF.7、MV.1以及全球主要流行株XEC.4、LP.8.1和XFG的血清中和试验。结果 共检测了5115名患者,其中171名(3.4%)严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2检测呈阳性。最常见的症状是咳嗽(91.7%)、鼻分泌物(74.5%)和发热(55.4%),而喘息(2%)和湿啰音(2.8%)是最少见的症状。测序结果显示JN.1奥密克戎亚系、LF.7、MV.1和XEF在传播。大多数感染主要变异株LF.7和MV.1的个体血清能够中和这些变异株,但无法中和近期流行的XFG变异株,其中23%(7/30)的血清未能中和XFG,表明仅感染JN.1系病毒不足以防止XFG的再感染。观察到接种疫苗和未接种疫苗个体的血清中和滴度分布有重叠,且对任何变异株均未观察到统计学意义上的显著差异。结论 本研究显示严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2在肯尼亚沿海地区持续传播,主要引起轻度上呼吸道疾病,而进化则显示出免疫逃逸能力。这些发现强调了整合基因组学和免疫学监测的重要性,以追踪新兴变异株、评估群体保护水平,并为未来的疫苗更新策略和公共卫生准备提供依据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
疫情后,严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2在非洲被优先级降低,突显了了解其持续传播和免疫逃逸潜力的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究于 2024 年 11 月至 2026 年 3 月在 Kilifi 健康人口统计和 监测 系统内的五个卫生机构进行,对出现急性呼吸道症状的患者进行 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RT-PCR 检测。
核心方法
通过采集鼻咽/咽拭子进行RT-PCR检测,对严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2进行了免疫监测。从30名经确认感染严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2的患者中采集康复期血浆,用于针对LF.7、MV.1、XEC.4、LP.8.1和XFG变异株的血清中和试验。
主要结果
在5115名检测患者中,有171名(3.4%)严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2检测呈阳性。血清中和试验显示,感染LF.7和MV.1变异株的个体血清能够中和这些变异株,但无法中和近期流行的XFG变异株,其中23%(7/30)的血清未能中和XFG。
解释与创新
本研究突显了严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2在肯尼亚沿海地区的持续传播,新兴的JN.1亚系显示出免疫逃逸能力,表明需要更新疫苗策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了维持基因组和免疫学监测监测的重要性,以追踪新兴变异株并评估群体保护水平,这对公共卫生准备和疫苗开发至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其关注特定地理区域以及使用小样本进行血清中和测定而受到限制,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Integrated surveillance in Kilifi reveals continued SARS-CoV-2 circulation and immune escape of emerging JN.1 sublineages post-pandemic

Authors: Gloria Konyino, Bernadette Kutima, Sharon Owuor, Donwilliams Omuoyo, Gerald Waweru, Igor Andrade Santos, Arnold W. Lambisia, Esther Nyadzua Katama, Angela Maina, Joyce Uche Nyiro
Original abstract (English)
Introduction SARS-CoV-2 has been deprioritized post-pandemic, especially in Africa. Vaccination in Kenya was conducted using the Wuhan-based vaccines only, which showed reduced neutralization efficacy against Omicron and its sublineages. Methods We performed SARS-CoV-2 immune surveillance in five health facilities within the Kilifi Health Demographic and Surveillance System from November 2024 to March 2026. Patients in the facilities presenting acute respiratory symptoms were recruited and nasopharyngeal/oropharyngeal swabs taken for SARS-CoV-2 RT-PCR. Convalescent plasma from 30 patients with confirmed SARS-CoV-2 RT-PCR positive tests and genome sequences were taken for sero-neutralization assays against the infecting variants LF.7, MV.1, and globally predominant strains, XEC.4, LP.8.1 and XFG. Results In total 5115 patients were tested and 171 patients (3.4%) were positive for SARS-CoV-2. Cough (91.7%), nasal discharge (74.5%) and fever (55.4%) were the most common symptoms while wheezing (2%) and crackles (2.8%) were rarest. Sequencing revealed circulation of JN.1 omicron sub-lineages, LF.7, MV.1 and XEF. Sera from majority of individuals infected by the dominant variants, LF.7 and MV.1 were able to neutralize infecting variants but not the recently circulating XFG variant 23% (7/30) suggesting that infection with a JN.1 lineage virus alone is not sufficient to prevent re-infection with XFG. Overlapping titer distributions was observed between vaccinated and unvaccinated sera, with no statistically significant difference observed for any variant. Conclusion Our study demonstrates that SARS-CoV-2 continues to circulate in coastal Kenya causing predominantly mild upper respiratory illness while evolution enabling immune escape. These findings emphasize the importance of integrated genomic and immunological surveillance to track emerging variants, evaluate population-level protection, and inform future vaccine update strategies and public health preparedness efforts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Post-pandemic, SARS-CoV-2 has been deprioritized, especially in Africa, highlighting the need to understand its continued circulation and immune escape potential.
Design and population
The study was conducted in five health facilities within the Kilifi Health Demographic and Surveillance System from November 2024 to March 2026, testing patients presenting acute respiratory symptoms for SARS-CoV-2 RT-PCR.
Core methods
SARS-CoV-2 immune surveillance was performed using nasopharyngeal/oropharyngeal swabs for RT-PCR, and convalescent plasma from 30 patients with confirmed infections was used for sero-neutralization assays against LF.7, MV.1, XEC.4, LP.8.1, and XFG variants.
Main results
Out of 5115 tested patients, 171 (3.4%) were positive for SARS-CoV-2. Sero-neutralization assays showed that sera from individuals infected with LF.7 and MV.1 could neutralize these variants but not the recently circulating XFG variant, with 23% (7/30) of sera failing to neutralize it.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the continued circulation of SARS-CoV-2 in coastal Kenya, with the emergence of JN.1 sublineages demonstrating immune escape capabilities, suggesting the need for updated vaccine strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of maintaining genomic and immunological surveillance to monitor emerging variants and assess population-level protection, which is critical for public health preparedness and vaccine development.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a specific geographic region and the use of a small sample size for sero-neutralization assays, which may affect the generalizability of results. The evidence strength is moderate due to these constraints.

A Cox-based multistate model for disease progression using large-scale population-based registry data: Application to COVID-19

A Cox-based multistate model for disease progression using large-scale population-based registry data: Application to COVID-19
作者： Arostegui I, Klaus Langohr, Irantzu Barrio, Nere Larrea-Agirre, José M. Quintana, Guadalupe Gómez-Melis, Inmaculada Aróstegui
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to demonstrate the utility of multistate models (MSMs) in analyzing complex, real-world systematic registries to provide a comprehensive description of disease progression in COVID-19.
设计与对象
This study employed a Cox-based multistate model to analyze trajectories in patients with COVID-19 using population-based registry data. The dataset included 377,285 adults diagnosed between March 2020 and January 2022, spanning four pandemic periods and the vaccination rollout.
核心方法
该研究使用基于 Cox 的多状态模型,定义了八种状态和十一种转换,并结合了非参数概率估计和特定于转换的 Cox 模型。它包括社会人口统计学、合并症(查尔森指数)、疗养院居住、社会剥夺和时间依赖性免疫状态变量。
主要结果
主要研究结果表明,男性、年龄较大、高合并症指数和社会剥夺显着增加住院和 死亡率 风险。疫苗接种 和大流行的进展与恢复率的提高和严重程度的降低有关。医疗保健系统似乎随着时间的推移而发生变化,从最初的高 死亡率 过渡到更有选择性的 ICU 使用,并在后期提高了生存率。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明了 MSM 在模拟复杂的动态医疗过程方面的有效性,为疾病 监测 以及传染病和慢性病的政策制定提供了稳健且可解释的方法。
科研与公卫意义
The findings can inform surveillance strategies, risk assessment, and public health planning by highlighting key risk factors and the impact of vaccination on disease progression in COVID-19.
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对登记数据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差或缺失信息。然而,该方法是稳健且可转移的,对其准确模拟疾病进展的能力充满信心。

A Cox-based multistate model for disease progression using large-scale population-based registry data: Application to COVID-19

Authors: Arostegui I, Klaus Langohr, Irantzu Barrio, Nere Larrea-Agirre, José M. Quintana, Guadalupe Gómez-Melis, Inmaculada Aróstegui
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background and Objective: Multistate models (MSM) provide a dynamic framework to represent disease evolution through transitions between states: diagnosis, hospitalization, ICU admission, discharge, and death, enabling the estimation of transition probabilities and covariate effects. The primary goal was to demonstrate the utility of MSMs in analyzing complex, real-world systematic registries to provide a comprehensive description of disease progression. Methods: This study employs a Cox-based MSM to analyze trajecto-ries in patients with COVID-19 using population-based registry data. The dataset comprised 377,285 adults diagnosed between March 2020 and Jan-uary 2022, spanning four pandemic periods and the vaccination rollout. We included sociodemographics, comorbidities (Charlson Index), nursing home residency, social deprivation, and time-dependent immunization status variables. The model defined eight states and eleven transitions, utilizing non-parametric probability estimations and transition-specific Cox models. Results: Key findings indicate that male sex, older age, high comorbid-ity index, and social deprivation significantly increased hospitalization and mortality risks. Conversely, vaccination and the progression of the pandemic were associated with improved recovery rates and reduced severity. Furthermore , the healthcare system seemed to change over time: transitioning from high initial mortality to more selective ICU utilization and improved survival in later stages. Conclusions: MSMs proved highly effective for modeling complex dynamic medical processes and supporting strategic clinical and public health planning. Despite the inherent complexities of managing large datasets, this approach is highly transferable and offers a robust, interpretable method for disease surveillance and policy-making for both infectious and chronic conditions .
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to demonstrate the utility of multistate models (MSMs) in analyzing complex, real-world systematic registries to provide a comprehensive description of disease progression in COVID-19.
Design and population
This study employed a Cox-based multistate model to analyze trajectories in patients with COVID-19 using population-based registry data. The dataset included 377,285 adults diagnosed between March 2020 and January 2022, spanning four pandemic periods and the vaccination rollout.
Core methods
The study used a Cox-based multistate model that defined eight states and eleven transitions, incorporating non-parametric probability estimations and transition-specific Cox models. It included sociodemographics, comorbidities (Charlson Index), nursing home residency, social deprivation, and time-dependent immunization status variables.
Main results
Key findings indicated that male sex, older age, high comorbidity index, and social deprivation significantly increased hospitalization and mortality risks. Vaccination and the progression of the pandemic were associated with improved recovery rates and reduced severity. The healthcare system appeared to change over time, transitioning from high initial mortality to more selective ICU utilization and improved survival in later stages.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating the effectiveness of MSMs in modeling complex dynamic medical processes, offering a robust and interpretable method for disease surveillance and policy-making for both infectious and chronic conditions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance strategies, risk assessment, and public health planning by highlighting key risk factors and the impact of vaccination on disease progression in COVID-19.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on registry data, which may introduce biases or missing information. However, the approach is robust and transferable, with high confidence in its ability to model disease progression accurately.
